Let's design specific algorithm:

Global parameters:
G_N, G_ALPHA, G_PEN, G_LAM, G_DX, G_TARGET
Functions:
- project_feasible(h, n): clip, iterative projection to sum target. Could do faster: adjust by mass on values with room, but iterative okay.
- compute_counts(h,n): returns C array of length 2n-1? We can return P,Q normalized? For objective, want normalized c = counts * dx. To avoid dx in gradient, use counts and multiply objective by dx. Use counts.
  Using aut = np.correlate(h, h, 'full') [n-1:] Hpos.
  prefix = np.cumsum(np.r_[0,h])? prefix_len[L] = sum h[:L]. For d=0..n-1, L=n-d.
  P = prefix[L] - Hpos[d]
  suffix = total - prefix[d] = sum h[d:]

7. Try different n (e.g., 200, 240, 400) by interpolation from best and optimize less (short time).
8. Final evaluation and return.

Project feasibility:
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
 target=n/2
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
     room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
     if tot<1e-15: break
     h += diff/tot*room
   else:
     tot=h.sum()
     if tot<1e-15: break
     h += diff/tot*h
 np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
 return h
If after loop sum not exact, can do final: target2=target-h.sum(); if abs small, adjust on interior? Use iterative. For exact, final: if sum not exact, adjust values not at bounds: sort? The evaluation via np.correlate doesn't require exact sum? Constraint requires. The harness may check? It says sum exactly. We need ensure h values binary? Floating sum maybe 150.000000. Use adjust:
 while not close: diff; idx interior (0<h<1); if exists h[idx]+=diff/len; else clip.
But objective may use not exactly. We'll do loop to 1e-14.

Compute counts:
def compute_counts(h,n):
 # h 1d
 v? total = np.dot(h,h)
 H=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')[n-1:n] # length n H_d for d=0..n-1? Need verify index orientation.
 prefix=np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(h)))
 L=n - np.arange(n)
 P=prefix[L] - H
 suffix = prefix[n] - prefix[:n] # length n? suffix[d]=sum h[d:]
 Q=suffix - H
 # C counts: P[0] plus P[1:] plus Q[1:]
 C=np.empty(2*n-1)
 C[0]=P[0]
 C[1:n+1]=P[1:]
 C[n+1:]=Q[1:]
 return C

   if wpd==0 and wqd==0: continue
   L=n-d

      if wqd: grad[:L] -= wqd*seg

   if wpd: grad[d:n] -= wpd*seg2
   if wqd: grad[d:n] += wqd*(1.0-seg2)
 return grad

def smooth_sequence(h,n,deadline):
 h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=get_c5(h)
 # alpha schedule maybe [200, 1000, 4000]
 for alpha in (200,1000,4000):
   if time.time()>deadline: break

   # outer augmented up to 2 passes, each 150 iters? But too long.

     lam = lam + pen*(x.sum()-n/2)
     c=get_c5(x)
     if c<best_c: h,best_c=x,c
   # after high alpha, maybe coordinate exact
 return h,best_c

Function `smooth_common(h)` returns val,w,g. It will call compute_counts. We can make it top-level.
Pseudo:
G_ALPHA=1000.0; G_PEN=1.0; G_DX=2/300; G_TARGET=150
def smooth_common(h):
 C, maxc = get_counts(h)
 A = G_ALPHA * C
 mx = A.max()
 E = np.exp(A - mx)
 S = E.sum()
 w = E / S
 val = (mx + np.log(S)) / G_ALPHA
 g = h.sum() - G_TARGET
 return val,w,g
def _obj(x):
 val,w,g = smooth_common(x)
 return val*G_DX + G_PEN*g*g
def _jac(x):
 val,w,g=smooth_common(x)
 grad = G_DX * weighted_grad(w,x)
 grad += (2.0*G_PEN*g)*np.ones_like(x)
 return grad

`weighted_grad(w,h)`:
 n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n)
 wp=w[:n]; wq=w[n:]
 grad += wp[0]*(1-2*h)
 for d in range(1,n):
   wpd=wp[d]; wqd=wq[d-1]
   if not (wpd or wqd): continue
   L=n-d
   seg=h[d:d+L]
   if wpd !=0: grad[:L] += wpd*(1-seg)
   if wqd !=0: grad[:L] -= wqd*seg
   seg2=h[:L]
   if wpd !=0: grad[d:] -= wpd*seg2
   if wqd !=0: grad[d:] += wqd*(1-seg2)
 return grad

Local optimize function:
def smooth_optimize(h,n,deadline,alphas=(200,1500),maxiter=300):
 h=project_feasible(h,n); best=evaluate_c5(h); cur=h; lam? Use penalty only.
 for alpha in alphas:
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   G_ALPHA=alpha; G_PEN=1.0; G_DX=2.0/n; G_TARGET=n/2.0
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   # maybe restart with projected current
   try:
      res=minimize(_obj, cur.copy(), jac=_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':5000,'disp':False})
      x=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=evaluate_c5(x)
      if c<best: best,c; cur=x
   except Exception: pass
   # second pass with higher alpha? already alphas schedule.
 return cur,best

Let's detail functions code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_ALPHA=1.0
G_PEN=1.0
G_DX=1.0
G_TARGET=0.0

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    n=int(n)
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13:
            break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    # final adjust interior
    for _ in range(20):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
        cnt=mask.sum()
        if cnt==0: break
        h[mask] += diff/cnt
        np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
This may still not exact to 1e-12 due clipping. Good enough.

`compute_counts` as above. Need `np.correlate` returns float. For n=1? not.
```
def compute_counts(h,n):
    H=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')[n-1:2*n-1]
    pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(h,out=pref[1:]);
    L=n-np.arange(n)
    P=pref[L]-H
    Q=pref[n]-pref[:-n?]? Wait suffix = total - pref[d]. pref length n+1, pref[d] sum h[:d]. For d=0..n-1, suffix = pref[n]-pref[d]. We need array suffix length n: pref[n] - pref[:n].
    Q=pref[n]-pref[:n] - H
    C=np.empty(2*n-1); C[0]=P[0]; C[1:n+1]=P[1:]; C[n+1:]=Q[1:]
    return C
```
Check `pref[:n]` length n, yes. `pref[n]` scalar. For d=0 Q[0]=total -0 -H0 = total - sum h²? But Q0 not used? C[0]=P0, Q0 same; Q0 = total - H0 = P0? P0=pref[n]-H0. yes. Good.
`L=n-np.arange(n)` gives [n,n-1,...,1]. `pref[L]` sum h[:L]. Good.

`eval_c5`:
```
def eval_c5(h):
 n=len(h); return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*2.0/n)
```

`weighted_grad` as above. Need use `wq = w[n:2*n-1]`. If n=1, length0 but n>1.
```
def weighted_grad(w,h):
 n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n)
 wp=w[:n]; wq=w[n:]
 grad += wp[0]*(1.0-2.0*h)
 for d in range(1,n):
   wpd=wp[d]; wqd=wq[d-1]
   if wpd == 0.0 and wqd == 0.0: continue
   L=n-d
   seg=h[d:d+L]
   if wpd:
      grad[:L] += wpd
      grad[:L] -= wpd*seg
   if wqd:
      grad[:L] -= wqd*seg
   seg2=h[:L]
   if wpd:
      grad[d:] -= wpd*seg2
   if wqd:
      grad[d:] += wqd
      grad[d:] -= wqd*seg2
 return grad
```
This avoids some temps? Still. Condition `if wpd:` with tiny floats okay. Might be costly due Python branch. Use if `wpd > 1e-300`? Not.

`smooth_common`:
```
def smooth_common(h):
 C=compute_counts(h,len(h))
 A=G_ALPHA*C
 mx=A.max()
 E=np.exp(A-mx)
 S=E.sum()
 val=(mx+np.log(S))/G_ALPHA
 g=h.sum()-G_TARGET
 return val,C,E/S,g
```
If G_ALPHA=0? no. For very large alpha, if C differences NaN? no.

`_obj`,`_jac` use global.
```
def _obj(x):
 val,w,g=smooth_common(x)
 return val*G_DX + G_PEN*g*g
def _jac(x):
 val,w,g=smooth_common(x)
 grad=G_DX*weighted_grad(w,x)
 grad += 2.0*G_PEN*g
 return grad
```
Adding scalar to grad modifies all? `grad += scalar` works, yes.

Set globals in smooth_optimize:
```
def smooth_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=(300.0,2000.0),maxit=150):
 h=project_feasible(h,n); best=eval_c5(h)
 for alpha in alphas:
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   G_ALPHA=alpha; G_PEN=1.0; G_DX=2.0/n; G_TARGET=n/2.0
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try:
      res=minimize(_obj,h.copy(),jac=_jac,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':5*maxit,'disp':False})
      x=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      c=eval_c5(x)
      if c<best:
         h=x; best=c
   except Exception:
      pass
 return h,best
```
Global G variables are assigned, Python global needed: inside function, assigning G_ALPHA requires `global G_ALPHA, G_PEN, G_DX, G_TARGET`. Include at top of smooth_refine.

`cd_refine`:
```
def cd_refine(h,n,deadline,scales=(0.25,0.1,0.03,0.008),grid=100,penalty=0.05):
 h=project_feasible(h,n)
 best=eval_c5(h); best_h=h.copy()
 d=np.arange(1,n)
 rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
 for scale in scales:
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   # adapt grid maybe
   C=compute_counts(h,n); sumh=float(h.sum()); target=n/2
   cmax=float(np.max(C))
   bestval=cmax+penalty*(sumh-target)**2
   # if after project c lower update
   c=eval; if c<best...
   passes=0
   while time.time()<deadline:
     improved=False
     order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
     for p in order:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       a0=h[p]
       lo=max(-a0, -scale)
       hi=min(1.0-a0, scale)
       if hi-lo<1e-14: continue
       aP=np.zeros(n-1); aQ=np.zeros(n-1)
       L1=n-1-p
       if L1>0:
         seg=h[p+1:p+L1+1]
         aP[:L1]=1.0-seg
         aQ[:L1]=-seg
       if p>0:
         rev=h[:p][::-1]
         aP[:p] += -rev
         aQ[:p] += 1.0-rev
       a=np.empty(2*n-1)
       a[0]=1.0-2.0*a0
       a[1:n]=aP
       a[n:]=aQ
       # deltas
       # Use grid points; maybe n_grid = grid if n<=350 else 60
       dg=grid if n<=350 else 60
       deltas=np.linspace(lo,hi,dg)
       # include zero if not close
       if abs(deltas[0])>1e-15 or abs(deltas[-1])? 0 within. To include, we can do:
       # choose if 0 not equal to any, insert. Simpler: deltas=np.concatenate(([0.0],deltas)); deltas=np.unique(deltas) # may be unsorted? unique sorts.
       # But unique on 100 elements okay.
       deltas=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0],deltas)))
       # if length > 2
       if len(deltas) < 3: continue
       # For performance, if range small, use more? already.
       new = C[None, :] + deltas[:, None] * a[None, :]
       new[:, 0] -= deltas[:, None] * deltas
       vals = new.max(axis=1)
       s = sumh + deltas - target
       vals += penalty * s * s
       idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
       d_val = float(deltas[idx])
       if abs(d_val) > 1e-13 and vals[idx] < bestval - 1e-12:
           C += d_val * a
           C[0] -= d_val * d_val
           h[p] = a0 + d_val
           sumh += d_val
           cmax = float(np.max(C))
           bestval = cmax + penalty*(sumh-target)**2
           improved = True
     passes += 1
     if not improved:
       break
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   c=eval_c5(h)
   if c < best:
       best=c; best_h=h.copy()
 return best_h,best
```
Potential bug: after C updates, C may have small negative due to errors? no.
`bestval` includes penalty; after applying update, recompute cmax but C max. Good.
`new[:, 0] -= deltas[:, None] * deltas` broadcasting: `new[:,0]` shape (G,), `deltas[:,None]*deltas` shape (G, G)? Wait new[:,0] is view length G. `deltas[:,None] * deltas` multiplies column vector by row vector -> 2D GxG. That's wrong. Need `new[:, 0] -= deltas * deltas`. Since both 1D. Use that.
`new = C[None,:]+deltas[:,None]*a[None,:]`: C shape (N,), result (G,N). fine.
`vals = new.max(axis=1)`.
`deltas=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0],deltas)))` might change dtype; okay. If lo/hi large and grid, unique sorts. If 0 already, no duplicate.
`C += d_val * a` modifies all C; but for coordinate p, a was computed from old h; after update, C exact. Good.
But if d_val is at bound, h[p] may be -1e-16 due float; clip? We can `h[p]=float(np.clip(a0+d_val,0,1))`. But C update assumes no clip; if tiny outside, adjust? Use only if within [lo,hi], by construction; floating may -1e-16. Clip and if changed, recompute C? better not clip unless near bounds: `h[p]=max(0,min(1,a0+d_val))`. If clipped, C not consistent; but delta chosen within lo/hi so no. Use `h[p]=a0+d_val`.
Potential issue: `C` values could drift and max(c) maybe negative? no.
Penalty encourages sum but not exact. The coordinate pass may cause sum drift up to n*passes*scale? But if no improvement, break; if many moves same direction, sum could drift. Penalty prevents large. We can periodically if abs(sumh-target)>0.5 reproject and recompute C? But projection may break C; maybe okay. Add after each scale.

`fourier_search`:
```
def make_basis(n,M):
 x=2.0*np.arange(n)/n
 b=np.empty((M,n))
 for m in range(1,M+1): b[m-1]=np.cos(np.pi*m*x)
 return b
def fourier_vec(a,basis):
 n=basis.shape[1]
 return project_feasible(0.5 + basis.dot(a), n)
def fourier_obj(a,basis):
 a=np.clip(a,-0.6,0.6)
 return eval_c5(fourier_vec(a,basis))
def fourier_refine(a0,basis,n,deadline):
 a=np.clip(a0,-0.6,0.6).copy()
 best=eval_c5(fourier_vec(a,basis)); best_a=a.copy()
 # random? deterministic
 if time.time()<deadline-5:
   try:
     res=minimize(fourier_obj,a,args=(basis,),method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':300,'maxfev':800,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-7,'disp':False})
     x=res.x
   except: x=a
   # Also do a quick local with Powell? maybe no.
   c=eval_c5(fourier_vec(np.clip(x,-0.6,0.6),basis))
   if c<best: best=c; best_a=np.clip(x,...)
 # optional second start with a_sine if not? handled outside.
 return fourier_vec(best_a,basis), best
```
Nelder-Mead maxfev 800 okay. But `fourier_obj` calls project each eval; if raw h outside, projection 80 loops; 800*80*300 ~19M, okay.

`fourier_coeffs`:
```
def fourier_coeffs(h,basis,n):
 a=np.zeros(basis.shape[0])
 xm=h-0.5
 for m in range(len(a)): a[m]=2.0/n*np.dot(xm,basis[m])
 return np.clip(a,-0.6,0.6)
```

`interp`:
```
def interp_h(h,nnew):
 xold=2.0*np.arange(len(h))/len(h)
 xnew=2.0*np.arange(nnew)/nnew
 return project_feasible(np.interp(xnew,xold,h),nnew)
```

Run detailed timeline:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10.0
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try:
   h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
 except Exception:
   n0=300; h0=make_fallback(n0)
 h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
 best_h=h0; best_c=eval_c5(best_h)
 # sym
 hs=project_feasible(0.5*(h0+h0[::-1]),n0)
 cs=eval_c5(hs); if cs<best_c: update
 # CD initial (up to min(180, deadline*0.2))
 if time.time()<deadline-100:
   ctime = min(time.time()+120, deadline-180)
   h1,c1=cd_refine(best_h,n0,ctime,scales=(0.25,0.1,0.03),grid=100)
   if c1<best_c: update
 # Fourier
 if time.time()<deadline-180:
   basis=make_basis(n0,6)
   # candidate a from best
   a1=fourier_coeffs(hs,basis,n0)
   ft=min(90, deadline-time.time()-180)
   hf,cf=fourier_refine(a1,basis,n0,time.time()+ft)
   if cf<best_c: update
   a2=np.zeros(6); a2[0]=-0.5
   if time.time()<deadline-120:
      hf,cf=fourier_refine(a2,basis,n0,time.time()+min(60, deadline-time.time()-120))
      update
 # Smooth L-BFGS on best (maybe two alpha)
 if time.time()<deadline-120:
   maxit=250 if n0>350 else 350
   h2,c2=smooth_refine(best_h,n0,time.time()+min(200, deadline-120), alphas=(200,1500), maxit=maxit)
   update
 # try n 400 and 240 if time
 for nn in (400,240,360):
   if time.time()>deadline-150: break
   if nn==n0: continue
   hnew=interp_h(best_h,nn)
   # maybe sym
   hnew=project_feasible(0.5*(hnew+hnew[::-1]),nn)
   max_sec=min(100, max(30, deadline-time.time()-120))
   hopt,copt=smooth_refine(hnew,nn,time.time()+max_sec, alphas=(300,), maxit=150)
   # quick CD
   if time.time()<deadline-80:
      hopt,copt=cd_refine(hopt,nn,time.time()+min(40, deadline-80), scales=(0.1,0.03), grid=60)
   if copt<best_c: update
 # final polish high alpha + CD on best
 if time.time()<deadline-60:
   n=len(best_h)
   hfin,cfin=smooth_refine(best_h,n,time.time()+min(120, deadline-60), alphas=(3000,), maxit=300)
   update
 if time.time()<deadline-30:
   n=len(best_h)
   hfin,cfin=cd_refine(best_h,n,time.time()+min(90, deadline-30), scales=(0.1,0.03,0.008,0.003), grid=120)
   update
 best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
 best_c=eval_c5(best_h)
 return best_h,best_c,len(best_h)
```

for pert in [0.005,0.01,0.02]:
 if time<deadline-120:
   hp=project_feasible(best_h+rng.normal(0,pert,n))
   hp, cp = cd_refine(hp,n,time+60,...)
   if cp<best: update
```

Could also try the reverse of h? If C same? Not needed.

Let's refine code for CD performance:
- Precompute `d` not needed. Reusing `a` arrays per p; allocate aP,aQ each p. Could allocate once and zero? But p length changes. Allocation 300*2 arrays of 299 -> small.
- `deltas=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], np.linspace(lo,hi,dg))))`: This costs. We can create `deltas=np.linspace(lo,hi,dg)`; if not include zero, insert with `idx=np.searchsorted(deltas,0.0); deltas=np.insert(deltas,idx,0.0)`. Faster. `np.insert` alloc. Use unique okay.
- `new = C + deltas[:,None]*a` uses broadcasting; for high alpha? Fine.
But `C` updates in place; `new` uses current C. Good.

Potential bug with C length and a layout: Let's verify C length: C[0]=P0, C[1:n]=P1..P_{n-1}, C[n:2n-1]=Q1..Q_{n-1}. In cd, aP length n-1 assigned a[1:n] (indices 1..n-1). aQ length n-1 assigned a[n:] (indices n..2n-2). a[0]=P0. Good.
Derivative for P_d at p:
 P_d = sum_{i=0}^{L-1} h_i - h_i h_{i+d}. Derivative wrt h_p:
 If p < L: from h_p term: 1 - h_{p+d}; from product if p as h? yes.
 If p >= d: from -h_{p-d} h_p term: -h_{p-d}.
If both, sum. Our aP first L1 = n-1-p. For d ≤ L1, p+d <n, set 1-h[p+d]. For p>0, d≤p, add -h[p-d]. If L1 and p overlap, both added. Good.
Q_d = sum h_{i+d}(1-h_i): derivative:
 If p < L: -h[p+d] (p as i in 1-h_i)
 If p>=d: 1-h[p-d] (p as i+d).
Our aQ: first L1 = -h[p+d]; second p =1-h[p-d]. Good.

But C[0] is P0=Q0? In full correlation, there is only one lag0 value ∑h(1-h), not two. Derivative (1-2h), quadratic -δ². Good.

In cd, `new[:,0] -= deltas*deltas` after adding linear. If d negative, subtract positive. Correct.

Potential issue: For d=0, P0 derivative 1-2h and -d²; sum target penalty. Good.

Now, L-BFGS gradient for C layout: Weight wp[d] for P_d, wq[d-1] for Q_d. We used region p=0..L-1 and p=d..n-1. But note for p in p<L and p>=d both, derivative sums; our code adds region A vector and region B vector, which for p overlapping adds both. Good.

For P_d derivative region A: +wpd*(1-h[p+d]) - wqd*h[p+d]. Region B: -wpd*h[p-d] + wqd*(1-h[p-d]). Good.

Let's test gradient of P0: C[0] weight wp[0]; derivative 1-2h. Good.

One subtle issue: Our smooth objective uses C array which has duplicate P0? No, only one P0. The full np.correlate has one lag0. Good.

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
GA=GP=GDx=GT=GDL=0.0
def pf(h,n):
 h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(); target=n*0.5; np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
 for _ in range(80):
  d=target-h.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-13: break
  if d>0:
   r=1-h; s=r.sum()
   if s<1e-15: break
   h+=d/s*r
  else:
   s=h.sum()
   if s<1e-15: break
   h+=d/s*h
 np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
 for _ in range(30):
  d=target-h.sum()
  if abs(d)<1e-14: break
  m=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12); c=m.sum()
  if c==0: break
  h[m]+=d/c; np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
 return h

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

GA = 0.0
GP = 1.0
GDx = 1.0
GT = 0.0
GDL = 0.0


def _cb(xk):
    return time.time() > GDL


def pf(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = n * 0.5
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    for _ in range(80):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (d / s) * r
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (d / s) * h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    for _ in range(40):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-14:
            break
        m = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        c = int(m.sum())
        if c == 0:
            break
        h[m] += d / c
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def cc(h, n):
    H = np.correlate(h, h, mode='full')[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    pref = np.empty(n + 1)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    L = n - np.arange(n)
    P = pref[L] - H
    Q = pref[n] - pref[:n] - H
    C = np.empty(2 * n - 1)
    C[0] = P[0]
    C[1:n + 1] = P[1:]
    C[n + 1:] = Q[1:]
    return C


def ec(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))


def wgrad(w, h):
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    wp = w[:n]
    wq = w[n:]
    g += wp[0] * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    for d in range(1, n):
        a = wp[d]
        b = wq[d - 1]
        if a == 0.0 and b == 0.0:
            continue
        L = n - d
        x = h[d:d + L]
        if a:
            g[:L] += a
            g[:L] -= a * x
        if b:
            g[:L] -= b * x
        y = h[:L]
        if a:
            g[d:] -= a * y
        if b:
            g[d:] += b
            g[d:] -= b * y
    return g


def sc_wh(x):
    C = cc(x, len(x))
    A = GA * C
    mx = A.max()
    E = np.exp(A - mx)
    s = E.sum()
    val = (mx + np.log(s)) / GA
    gsum = x.sum() - GT
    return val, E / s, gsum


def _obj(x):
    val, w, g = sc_wh(x)
    return val * GDx + GP * g * g


def _jac(x):
    val, w, g = sc_wh(x)
    grd = GDx * wgrad(w, x)
    grd += 2.0 * GP * g
    return grd


def smooth_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=(300.0, 1800.0), maxit=220):
    global GA, GP, GDx, GT, GDL
    h = pf(h, n)
    best = ec(h)
    GDL = float(deadline)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        GA = float(alpha)
        GP = 1.0
        GDx = 2.0 / n
        GT = 0.5 * n
        try:
            res = minimize(_obj, h.copy(), jac=_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                           options={'maxiter': maxit, 'maxfun': max(120, 2 * maxit),
                                    'ftol': 1e-15, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'disp': False,
                                    'callback': _cb})
            x = pf(res.x, n)
            c = ec(x)
            if c < best:
                h, best = x, c
        except Exception:
            pass
    return h, best


def cd_refine(h, n, deadline, scales=(0.25, 0.10, 0.03, 0.008), grid=90, pen=0.05):
    h = pf(h, n)
    best = ec(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    rng = np.random.RandomState(1234)
    for scale in scales:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        C = cc(h, n)
        sumh = float(h.sum())
        cmax = float(C.max())
        bestval = cmax + pen * (sumh - target) ** 2
        c = ec(h)
        if c < best:
            best, best_h = c, h.copy()
        dg = grid if n <= 360 else 60
        while time.time() < deadline:
            improved = False
            order = np.arange(n)
            rng.shuffle(order)
            for p in order:
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                a0 = h[p]
                lo = max(-a0, -scale)
                hi = min(1.0 - a0, scale)
                if hi - lo < 1e-14:
                    continue
                aP = np.zeros(n - 1)
                aQ = np.zeros(n - 1)
                L1 = n - 1 - p
                if L1 > 0:
                    seg = h[p + 1:p + L1 + 1]
                    aP[:L1] = 1.0 - seg
                    aQ[:L1] = -seg
                if p > 0:
                    rev = h[:p][::-1]
                    aP[:p] += -rev
                    aQ[:p] += 1.0 - rev
                a = np.empty(2 * n - 1)
                a[0] = 1.0 - 2.0 * a0
                a[1:n] = aP
                a[n:] = aQ
                deltas = np.linspace(lo, hi, dg)
                idx0 = int(np.searchsorted(deltas, 0.0))
                if abs(deltas[min(idx0, len(deltas) - 1)]) > 1e-16:
                    deltas = np.insert(deltas, idx0, 0.0)
                if len(deltas) < 3:
                    continue
                new = C[None, :] + deltas[:, None] * a[None, :]
                new[:, 0] -= deltas * deltas
                vals = new.max(axis=1)
                s = sumh + deltas - target
                vals += pen * s * s
                i = int(np.argmin(vals))
                dd = float(deltas[i])
                if abs(dd) > 1e-13 and vals[i] < bestval - 1e-12:
                    C += dd * a
                    C[0] -= dd * dd
                    h[p] = a0 + dd
                    sumh += dd
                    cmax = float(C.max())
                    bestval = cmax + pen * (sumh - target) ** 2
                    improved = True
            if not improved:
                break
        h = pf(h, n)
        c = ec(h)
        if c < best:
            best, best_h = c, h.copy()
    return best_h, best


def make_basis(n, M):
    x = 2.0 * np.arange(n) / n
    b = np.empty((M, n))
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        b[m - 1] = np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
    return b


def fv(a, basis):
    n = basis.shape[1]
    return pf(0.5 + basis.dot(np.clip(a, -0.6, 0.6)), n)


def fo(a, basis):
    return ec(fv(a, basis))


def fourier_refine(a0, basis, deadline):
    a = np.clip(a0, -0.6, 0.6).copy()
    hbest = fv(a, basis)
    cbest = ec(hbest)
    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        try:
            res = minimize(fo, a, args=(basis,), method='Nelder-Mead',
                           options={'maxiter': 300, 'maxfev': 900,
                                    'xatol': 1e-6, 'fatol': 1e-8, 'disp': False})
            x = np.clip(res.x, -0.6, 0.6)
        except Exception:
            x = a
        hx = fv(x, basis)
        cx = ec(hx)
        if cx < cbest:
            hbest, cbest = hx, cx
    return hbest, cbest


def ic(h, a, b, n):
    a = np.zeros(b.shape[0])
    x = h - 0.5
    for i in range(b.shape[0]):
        a[i] = (2.0 / n) * float(np.dot(x, b[i]))
    return np.clip(a, -0.6, 0.6)


def interp_h(h, nnew):
    xold = 2.0 * np.arange(len(h)) / len(h)
    xnew = 2.0 * np.arange(nnew) / nnew
    return pf(np.interp(xnew, xold, h), nnew)


def fallback_h(n):
    x = 2.0 * np.arange(n) / n
    return pf(0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x), n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 12.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 300
        h0 = fallback_h(n)
    h0 = pf(h0, n)
    best_h = h0
    best_c = ec(best_h)

    hs = pf(0.5 * (h0 + h0[::-1]), n)
    cs = ec(hs)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    if time.time() < deadline - 180.0:
        d1 = min(time.time() + 150.0, deadline - 220.0)
        h1, c1 = cd_refine(best_h, n, d1, scales=(0.30, 0.12, 0.04), grid=90)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
        M = 6
        basis = make_basis(n, M)
        a1 = ic(best_h, basis, None, n)
        d2 = min(time.time() + 80.0, deadline - 220.0)
        h2, c2 = fourier_refine(a1, basis, d2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
        a2 = np.zeros(M)
        a2[0] = -0.5
        if time.time() < deadline - 150.0:
            d3 = min(time.time() + 55.0, deadline - 150.0)
            h3, c3 = fourier_refine(a2 + rng.normal(0, 0.02, M), basis, d3)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h3, c3

    if time.time() < deadline - 160.0:
        d4 = min(time.time() + 180.0, deadline - 120.0)
        h4, c4 = smooth_refine(best_h, n, d4, alphas=(200.0, 1500.0),
                               maxit=260 if n <= 320 else 160)
        if c4 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h4, c4

    for nn in (400, 240, 360):
        if time.time() > deadline - 140.0:
            break
        if nn == n or nn > 800:
            continue
        hnew = interp_h(best_h, nn)
        hnew = pf(0.5 * (hnew + hnew[::-1]), nn)
        sec = min(70.0, max(25.0, deadline - time.time() - 110.0))
        ho, co = smooth_refine(hnew, nn, time.time() + sec, alphas=(400.0,), maxit=120)
        if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
            ho, co = cd_refine(ho, nn, min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 80.0),
                               scales=(0.12, 0.04), grid=60)
        if co < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = ho, co

    if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
        for p in (0.008, 0.018, 0.030):
            if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
                break
            hp = pf(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, p, len(best_h)), len(best_h))
            hp, cp = cd_refine(hp, len(best_h), min(time.time() + 35.0, deadline - 60.0),
                               scales=(0.08, 0.025, 0.008), grid=70)
            if cp < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = hp, cp

    if time.time() < deadline - 55.0:
        nn = len(best_h)
        h5, c5 = smooth_refine(best_h, nn, min(time.time() + 70.0, deadline - 40.0),
                               alphas=(3000.0,), maxit=260 if nn <= 320 else 140)
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h5, c5

    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
        nf = len(best_h)
        h6, c6 = cd_refine(best_h, nf, min(time.time() + 55.0, deadline - 8.0),
                           scales=(0.08, 0.025, 0.008, 0.003), grid=110)
        if c6 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h6, c6

    best_h = pf(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = ec(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), len(best_h)
```
